Prompt starts with: `chantier cr-driver for &lt;cr&gt; "&lt;title&gt;" of batch &lt;batch&gt;, in the project &lt;root&gt;.` ([gsemet/chantier](https://github.com/gsemet/chantier/blob/HEAD/agents/cr-driver.md) · ★2)
- **cr-econometric-reasoning**: Reviews structural econometric model logic: economic theory consistency, functional form appropriateness, distributional assumptions, estimation strategy selection (MLE vs GMM vs Bayesian), and assumption-data consistency. Loaded by /cr-review for Theory/Modeling tasks. ([GPID-WB/compound-gpid](https://github.com/GPID-WB/compound-gpid/blob/HEAD/.kilo/agents/cr-econometric-reasoning.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)

- **cr-finder**: Code-review finder for the code-review-max-windows workflow. Reviews one diff from ONE assigned angle and writes candidate findings as JSON. Loaded by /cr-review conditionally for tasks claiming identification. ([GPID-WB/compound-gpid](https://github.com/GPID-WB/compound-gpid/blob/HEAD/.kilo/agents/cr-identification-audit.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)

- **crab-medium**: Crab-flow medium worker (Opus, medium effort). Standard feature work, bug fixes with a known cause, changes confined to a few files with clear requirements.
